- (`P-003/P3-E007`) Import runs M-5 elai-verify against the bundle evidence, refuses on a failed receipt, then mounts the mechanism.
- (`P-003/P3-E009`) Sharing surface is admission-gated: every artifact carries a receipt and passes the same L-12/skillspector admission gates used for skills; friend-to-friend and marketplace share one gate.
- (`P-003/P3-E016`) M2: valid bundle re-verifies GREEN and mounts (`app_import` SSB); tampered mechanism or stale receipt is REFUSED with no mount.
- (`P-003/P3-E018`) FORK: if M-5 cannot yet verify a whole-app bundle, ship Tier-1 export + import-with-receipt-presence-check (refuse absent/malformed receipt), route full evidence re-verification to Tier-2; never ship a mount that skips verification entirely.
- (`P-003/P3-E020`) Production reachability is live `elai app export` / `elai app import` CLI; `#[cfg(test)]` never satisfies R7. PRODUCTION_HOOK is `cli/main.rs` subcommand dispatch.
- (`P-003/P3-A004`) Friend-to-friend/marketplace hosting UX is deferred; no Tier-1 UI beyond CLI is named.

- (`Q-001/QG-E021`) [SRC] class=`E/EVIDENCE` W-3 HypoSpace (arXiv:2510.15614) supports Validity/Uniqueness/Recovery in enumerable deterministic spaces; does not replace target execution value or grounding guards. _(anchor=QG:L53-L53; ledger=DEEP-Q-001.discovery.md:L56)_
